# Animation index

Every animation clip name, with the scripts that play it.

**3,813 distinct values** across 1156 scripts. Sorted by how many scripts use each: widely-shared values first, one-off values last.

A value used by exactly one script is that script's own; a value used by hundreds is shared plumbing.
